Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The analytical variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 258 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 726 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the analytical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^258 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 726-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
